rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Capital_punishment_in_Saudi_Arabia" title="Capital punishment in Saudi Arabia">Capital punishment</a>,<sup about="#mwt6" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-wp_ten_2016_1-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"wp ten 2016"}}'><a href="#cite_note-wp_ten_2016-1" id="mwCQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCw">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwDA">]</span></span></a></sup> prison terms (indeterminate length),<sup about="#mwt8" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-irb_Canada_2-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"irb Canada"}}'><a href="#cite_note-irb_Canada-2" id="mwDQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwDg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwDw">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwEA">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" same-sex relationships</td></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="infobox-label"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Same-sex_adoption" title="Same-sex adoption">Adoption</a></th><td class="infobox-data">No</td></tr></tbody></table>

<p id="mwEQ">Lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender and queer (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LGBTQ" title="LGBTQ" class="mw-redirect" id="mwEg">LGBTQ</a>) people in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saudi_Arabia" title="Saudi Arabia" id="mwEw">Saudi Arabia</a> face repression and discrimination.<sup about="#mwt11" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-PinkNews_3-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"PinkNews"}}'><a href="#cite_note-PinkNews-3" id="mwFA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwFQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFg">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFw">]</span></span></a></sup> Both male and female same-sex behavior is punishable by death within the country.<sup about="#mwt14" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ilga_2020_4-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ilga 2020"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ilga_2020-4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ilga_2020-4" id="mwGA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwGQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGg">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup class="reference nowrap" about="#mwt10" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwHA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"rp","href":"./Template:Rp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"135–136"}},"i":0}}]}'><span title="Page / location: 135–136"><span typeof="mw:Entity">:</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>135–136<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span></sup> Therefore, there are no protections for LGBTQ people in Saudi Arabia.</p>

<p id="mwHQ">The law of Saudi Arabia is uncodified; a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wahhabist" title="Wahhabist" class="mw-redirect" id="mwHg">Wahhabist</a> interpretation of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sharia" title="Sharia" id="mwHw">sharia</a>, derived from the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quran" title="Quran" id="mwIA">Quran</a> and the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sunnah" title="Sunnah" id="mwIQ">Sunnah</a>, is the basis of the law and justice system.<sup about="#mwt26" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-IDuA_5-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"IDuA"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-IDuA-5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-IDuA-5" id="mwIg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJA">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup class="reference nowrap" about="#mwt15" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwJg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"rp","href":"./Template:Rp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"205"}},"i":0}}]}'><span title="Page / location: 205"><span typeof="mw:Entity">:</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>205<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span></sup> In particular, the Quranic account of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lot_in_Islam" title="Lot in Islam" id="mwJw">prophet Lut</a> and certain teachings of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammad" title="Muhammad" id="mwKA">Muhammad</a> in the Sunnah, inform the legal treatment of same-sex sexual activity and nonconforming-gender presentation. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homosexuality" title="Homosexuality" id="mwKQ">Homosexuality</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Transgender" title="Transgender" id="mwKg">transgender</a> status or gender non-conformity are widely seen as immoral and indecent, and the law allows penalties for acts of homosexuality of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Capital_punishment" title="Capital punishment" id="mwKw">capital punishment</a>,<sup about="#mwt27" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ilga_2020_4-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ilga 2020"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ilga_2020-4" id="mwLA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwLQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLg">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup class="reference nowrap" about="#mwt16" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwMA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"rp","href":"./Template:Rp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"69–74"}},"i":0}}]}'><span title="Page / location: 69–74"><span typeof="mw:Entity">:</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>69–74<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span></sup><sup about="#mwt30" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-wp_ten_2016_1-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"wp ten 2016"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-wp_ten_2016-1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-wp_ten_2016-1" id="mwMQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwMg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwMw">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt33" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-pink_apr_2019_6-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"pink apr 2019"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-pink_apr_2019-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-pink_apr_2019-6" id="mwNQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwNg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNw">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOA">]</span></span></a></sup> prison sentences of indeterminate length<sup about="#mwt17" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"group":"lower-alpha"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-7"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"efn","href":"./Template:Efn"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"The length of any prison sentence imposed is discretionary, not codified, nor dependent on precedent. Maximum terms of imprisonment are not ascertainable,&lt;ref name=\"IDuA\"/> but [[life imprisonment|life terms]] may be possible."}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-7" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwOQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwOg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOw">[</span>a<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt36" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-irb_Canada_2-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"irb Canada"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-irb_Canada-2"}}'><a href="#cite_note-irb_Canada-2" id="mwPQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwPg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPw">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQA">]</span></span></a></sup> (the maximum term is unknown),<sup about="#mwt39" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ilga_frameworks_8-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ilga frameworks"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ilga_frameworks-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ilga_frameworks-8" id="mwQQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwQg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQw">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRA">]</span></span></a></sup> fines, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flogging" title="Flogging" class="mw-redirect" id="mwRQ">flogging</a>, and deportation for foreigners.<sup about="#mwt42" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-bbc_2015_flog_9-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"bbc 2015 flog"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-bbc_2015_flog-9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-bbc_2015_flog-9" id="mwRg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwRw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSA">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSQ">]</span></span></a></sup> During investigations and detentions, mistreatment of suspects and detainees, including beatings and torture, have occurred.<sup about="#mwt43" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-IDuA_5-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"IDuA"}}'><a href="#cite_note-IDuA-5" id="mwSg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTA">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup class="reference nowrap" about="#mwt23" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwTg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"rp","href":"./Template:Rp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"208–209"}},"i":0}}]}'><span title="Page / location: 208–209"><span typeof="mw:Entity">:</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>208–209<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span></sup> Community violence against LGBTQ persons occurs.<sup about="#mwt46" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-toys_10-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"toys"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-toys-10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-toys-10" id="mwTw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUQ">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt49" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-times_2016_wikileaks_11-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"times 2016 wikileaks"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-times_2016_wikileaks-11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-times_2016_wikileaks-11" id="mwUw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwVA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVQ">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwVw">In May 2023, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saudi_Tourism_Authority" title="Saudi Tourism Authority" id="mwWA">Saudi Tourism Authority</a> website updated its FAQ page stating that all visitors, including LGBTQ visitors, were welcome to visit the country.<sup about="#mwt52" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-12" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-12" id="mwWQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwWg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWw">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXA">]</span></span></a></sup> Though homosexuality remains illegal, enforcement of the laws is not universal.<sup about="#mwt55" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-13" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-13" id="mwXQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwXg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXw">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYA">]</span></span></a></sup> The move follows a series of social and economic changes, including the abandonment of the enforcement of wearing the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hijab" title="Hijab" id="mwYQ">hijab</a> in public,<sup about="#mwt58" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-14" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-14" id="mwYg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwYw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZA">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt61" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-15" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-15" id="mwZg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwZw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaA">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaQ">]</span></span></a></sup> and the opening of the first public beach in Saudi Arabia where women can wear bikinis.<sup about="#mwt64" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-16" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-16" id="mwag"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwaw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbA">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbQ">]</span></span></a></sup> The decision to host the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2034_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2034 FIFA World Cup" id="mwbg">2034 FIFA World Cup</a> within Saudi Arabia received strong criticism from the LGBTQ community.<sup about="#mwt67" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-PinkNews_3-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"PinkNews"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-PinkNews-3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-PinkNews-3" id="mwbw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwcA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcQ">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt70" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-17" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mwcw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwdA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwdQ">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwdg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwdw" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mweA" aria-labelledby="Legality"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Legality">Legality</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Saudi_Arabia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Legality"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mweQ" aria-labelledby="Criminal_laws"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Criminal_laws">Criminal laws</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Saudi_Arabia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Criminal laws"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt71" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mweg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"see also","href":"./Template:See_also"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Legal system of Saudi Arabia"},"2":{"wt":"LGBT in Islam"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1368532237" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt72" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'>.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+.mw-empty-elt+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}@media print{body.ns-0 .mw-parser-output .hatnote{display:none!important}}</style></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt71" id="mwew">See also: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Legal_system_of_Saudi_Arabia" title="Legal system of Saudi Arabia">Legal system of Saudi Arabia</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LGBT_in_Islam" title="LGBT in Islam" class="mw-redirect">LGBT in Islam</a></div>
<p id="mwfA">Saudi Arabia has no <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Criminal_code" title="Criminal code" id="mwfQ">criminal code</a>. The primary source of law in Saudi Arabia is the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sharia" title="Sharia" id="mwfg">Sharia</a> (Islamic law), which is derived from the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quran" title="Quran" id="mwfw">Quran</a>. Specifically, prohibition of same-sex sexual acts is based on the Quranic story of the prophet <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lot_in_Islam" title="Lot in Islam" id="mwgA">Lut</a> and traditions of the teachings of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammad" title="Muhammad" id="mwgQ">Muhammad</a> contained in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sunnah" title="Sunnah" id="mwgg">Sunnah</a>.<sup about="#mwt79" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Campbell_18-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Campbell"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Campbell-18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Campbell-18" id="mwgw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwhA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhQ">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhg">]</span></span></a></sup> <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt73" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwhw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"[[Ijma]]"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ijma" title="Ijma">Ijma</a></i></span>, or scholarly consensus on the meaning of the Quran and the Sunnah, developed after Muhammad's death. <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt74" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwiA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"[[Qiyas]]"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qiyas" title="Qiyas">Qiyas</a></i></span>, or analogical reasoning applied to the principles of the Quran, Sunnah and <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt75" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwiQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"ijma"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">ijma</i></span> is used:<sup about="#mwt82" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-19" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-19"}}'><a href="#cite_note-19" id="mwig"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwiw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjA">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjQ">]</span></span></a></sup> Judges apply their personal interpretation of sharia to any particular case (<i id="mwjg"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ijtihad" title="Ijtihad" id="mwjw">ijtihad</a></i>) and are not bound by any previous decisions, sometimes resulting in divergent judgements even in apparently identical cases.<sup about="#mwt85" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Otto161/2_20-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Otto161/2"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Otto161/2-20"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Otto161/2-20" id="mwkA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwkQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwkg">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwkw">]</span></span></a></sup> In addition, royal decrees and government regulations have been issued. Reformers have often called for codified laws to be instituted, and there appears to be a trend in the country to codify, publish and even translate some Saudi criminal and civil laws.<sup about="#mwt88" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-21" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-21"}}'><a href="#cite_note-21" id="mwlA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwlQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlg">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlw">]</span></span></a></sup> However, the traditional interpretation of Sharia is that it prohibits homosexual acts (as <i id="mwmA"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zina" title="Zina" id="mwmQ">zina</a></i> or unlawful sexual intercourse) and, specifically, <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt76" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwmg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"[[liwat]]"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liwat" title="Liwat" class="mw-redirect">liwat</a></i></span> or <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sodomy" title="Sodomy" id="mwmw">sodomy</a>, though there is a difference of opinion on the punishment ranging from none at all (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hanafi" title="Hanafi" class="mw-redirect" id="mwnA">Hanafi</a>) to the death penalty.<sup about="#mwt91" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-22" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-22"}}'><a href="#cite_note-22" id="mwnQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwng"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnw">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwoA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt94" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-23" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-23"}}'><a href="#cite_note-23" id="mwoQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwog"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwow">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt97" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-peters_24-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"peters"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-peters-24"}}'><a href="#cite_note-peters-24" id="mwpQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwpg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpw">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwqQ">In 1928, the Saudi judicial board advised Islamic judges to look for guidance in two books by the Hanbalite jurist <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mar'i_al-Karmi" title="Mar'i al-Karmi" id="mwqg">Marʿī ibn Yūsuf al-Karmī al-Maqdisī</a> (died 1033/1624). <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt98" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwqw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"Liwat"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">Liwat</i></span> (sodomy) is to be:</p>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1244412712" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt99" id="mwrA" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"...treated like [[fornication]], and must be punished in the same manner. If &apos;&apos;muḥṣan&apos;&apos; [commonly translated as \"adulterer\" but technically meaning someone who has had legal intercourse, but who may or may not currently be married] and free [not a slave], one must be stoned to death, while a free bachelor must be punished with 100 lashes and banished for a year."}},"i":0}}]}'>.mw-parser-output .templatequote{overflow:hidden;margin:1em 0;padding:0 32px}.mw-parser-output .templatequotecite{line-height:1.5em;text-align:left;margin-top:0}@media(min-width:500px){.mw-parser-output .templatequotecite{padding-left:1.6em}}</style><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt99" id="mwrQ"><p>...treated like <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fornication" title="Fornication">fornication</a>, and must be punished in the same manner. If <i>muḥṣan</i> [commonly translated as "adulterer" but technically meaning someone who has had legal intercourse, but who may or may not currently be married] and free [not a slave], one must be stoned to death, while a free bachelor must be punished with 100 lashes and banished for a year.</p></blockquote><p id="mwrg">

Sodomy is proven either by the perpetrator confessing four times or by the testimony of four trustworthy Muslim men, who were eyewitnesses to the act. If there are fewer than four witnesses, or if one is not upstanding, then they are all to be chastised with 80 lashes for slander.<sup about="#mwt106" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-25" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-25"}}'><a href="#cite_note-25" id="mwrw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwsA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsQ">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsg">]</span></span></a></sup> These safeguards contained in Sharia for capital crimes are not complied with, according to the ILGA. It considers that Saudi Arabia has<sup about="#mwt107" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ilga_2020_4-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ilga 2020"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ilga_2020-4" id="mwsw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwtA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwtQ">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwtg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup class="reference nowrap" about="#mwt101" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwtw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"rp","href":"./Template:Rp"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"73–74"}},"i":0}}]}'><span title="Page / location: 73–74"><span typeof="mw:Entity">:</span><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>73–74<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span></sup></p><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1244412712" about="#mwt102" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwuA" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"text":{"wt":"...{{nbsp}}failed to incorporate the procedural protections and safeguards that Sharia law has traditionally associated with the death penalty.{{nbsp}}... factors such as the religion, citizenship, and social status of the accused can be a strong determinant of the conviction and severity of punishments, with working-class migrant workers usually being judged and treated more harshly than upper-class Saudi citizens."},"2":{"wt":"ILGA (2020). \"Death Penalty: Saudi Arabia – Enforcement\". &apos;&apos;State-sponsored Homophobia&apos;&apos; report."}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt102"><p>...<span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span>failed to incorporate the procedural protections and safeguards that Sharia law has traditionally associated with the death penalty.<span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span>... factors such as the religion, citizenship, and social status of the accused can be a strong determinant of the conviction and severity of punishments, with working-class migrant workers usually being judged and treated more harshly than upper-class Saudi citizens.</p></blockquote><div class="templatequotecite" about="#mwt102" id="mwuQ"><p style="display: inline; padding-left: 2.3em;">—<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>ILGA (2020). "Death Penalty: Saudi Arabia – Enforcement". <i>State-sponsored Homophobia</i> report.<span style="display:none" data-plural="0"></span></p></div>

<p id="mwug">In the 1980s, Saudi King Khaled issued numerous royal decrees designed to secure support among religious fundamentalists in the aftermath of an uprising of religious extremists in 1979, known as the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grand_Mosque_seizure" title="Grand Mosque seizure" id="mwuw">Grand Mosque seizure</a>.</p>

<p id="mwvA">The "Rules of Apprehension, Temporary Custody &amp; Precautionary Detention Regulation" codified the criminal code on homosexuality by listing it among the crimes that warranted arrest and detention. In addition to law enforcement, a second royal decree formally established the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Committee_for_the_Promotion_of_Virtue_and_the_Prevention_of_Vice_(Saudi_Arabia)" title="Committee for the Promotion of Virtue and the Prevention of Vice (Saudi Arabia)" id="mwvQ">Committee for the Promotion of Virtue and the Prevention of Vice</a> (CPVPV) and gave this committee the power to arrest and detain people who violate the traditional teachings of Islam, including acts of homosexuality and cross-dressing.</p>

<p id="mwvg">Combating homosexuality remains one of the major goals of the CPVPV, along with its campaign against the consumption of alcohol and the practice of magic.<sup about="#mwt110" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2010_sg_26-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2010 sg"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-2010_sg-26"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2010_sg-26" id="mwvw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwwA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwwQ">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwwg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwww">In September 2013, it was announced that all <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_Cooperation_Council" title="Gulf Cooperation Council" id="mwxA">Gulf Cooperative Countries</a> had agreed to discuss a proposal to establish some form of, yet unknown, testing in order to ban gay foreigners from entering any of the countries.<sup about="#mwt113" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-27" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-27"}}'><a href="#cite_note-27" id="mwxQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwxg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxw">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt116" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-28" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-28"}}'><a href="#cite_note-28" id="mwyQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwyg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyw">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwzA">]</span></span></a></sup> However, it has been suggested that concern regarding the hosting of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2022 FIFA World Cup" id="mwzQ">2022 FIFA World Cup</a> by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qatar" title="Qatar" id="mwzg">Qatar</a>, and fears for the inevitable worldwide controversy if it is decided that football fans will have to be screened for homosexuality, made officials backtrack the plans and insist that it was a mere proposal.<sup about="#mwt119" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-29" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-29"}}'><a href="#cite_note-29" id="mwzw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw0A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0Q">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw0g">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mw0w">According to a transcript published by Saudi <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/State_media" title="State media" id="mw1A">state media</a> in March 2022, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Crown_Prince_of_Saudi_Arabia" title="Crown Prince of Saudi Arabia" id="mw1Q">Crown Prince</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mohammed_bin_Salman" title="Mohammed bin Salman" id="mw1g">Mohammed bin Salman</a>, the <i id="mw1w">de facto</i> ruler of Saudi Arabia, stated in an interview with <i id="mw2A"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Atlantic" title="The Atlantic" id="mw2Q">The Atlantic</a></i> magazine that the death penalty has been abolished in the country, except for instances of murder or when an individual poses a threat to many people.<sup about="#mwt122" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-30" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-30"}}'><a href="#cite_note-30" id="mw2g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw2w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3A">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3Q">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mw3g">In 2019, to coincide with the introduction of tourist visas to the country, the Saudi government decreed a code to regulate the behavior of Saudi citizens and foreigners in public places. The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_Decency_Law_in_Saudi_Arabia" title="Public Decency Law in Saudi Arabia" id="mw3w">new Public Decency Law</a> contains nineteen regulations, violations of which are subject to fines. Proscribed practices include public displays of affection, wearing immodest clothing, using profanity, and displaying immoral advertising. Some behaviors unrelated to sexual or gender expression are also covered; for example, littering, taking photographs and videos of people without permission, and queue jumping. The authority of the religious police to intervene in such matters has been reduced, with the general police force being the "sole authority responsible for monitoring offenses and imposing fines".<sup about="#mwt125" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-31" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-31"}}'><a href="#cite_note-31" id="mw4A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw4Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw4g">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw4w">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt128" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-32" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-32"}}'><a href="#cite_note-32" id="mw5A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw5Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5g">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5w">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mw6A" aria-labelledby="Enforcement_cases"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Enforcement_cases">Enforcement cases</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Saudi_Arabia&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Enforcement cases"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt129" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mw6Q" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Update section","href":"./Template:Update_section"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"December 2023"},"reason":{"wt":"All instances, except one, are before 2014. Of the ~ten cases here, eight occurred before 2010, making most of the &apos;enforcement&apos; examples given well over 12 years old"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1364180890" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt130" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Message box/ambox.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'>.mw-parser-output .ambox{border:1px solid #a2a9b1;border-left:10px solid #36c;background-color:#fbfbfb;box-sizing:border-box}.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+.mw-empty-elt+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+.mw-empty-elt+.mw-empty-elt+.ambox{margin-top:-1px}html body.mediawiki .mw-parser-output .ambox.mbox-small-left{margin:4px 1em 4px 0;overflow:hidden;width:238px;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:88%;line-height:1.25em}.mw-parser-output .ambox-speedy{border-left:10px .ambox-style{border-left:10px solid #fc3}.mw-parser-output .ambox-move{border-left:10px solid #9932cc}.mw-parser-output .ambox-protection{border-left:10px solid #a2a9b1}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-imageright{border:none;padding:2px 0.5em 2px 0;text-align:center}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-empty-cell{border:none;padding:0;width:1px}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-image-div{width:52px}@media(min-width:720px){.mw-parser-output .ambox{margin:0 10%}}@media print{body.ns-0 .mw-parser-output .ambox{display:none!important}}</style></span><table class="box-Update plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Update" role="presentation" about="#mwt129"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Ambox_current_red_Asia_Australia.svg"
